Extensive experiments demonstrate that with structurally independent text chunks, our methods significantly improve retrieval accuracy and context relevance over baselines, ultimately enhancing downstream QA performance, while ablation studies validate the efficacy of narratological guidance and data distillation.

</details>


### [76] [Formalization of Malagasy conjugation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27161)
*Joro Ny Aina Ranaivoarison,Eric Laporte,Baholisoa Simone Ralalaoherivony*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文报告了为马达加斯加语简单动词构建基于词典的形态分析器的核心语言学工作。使用Unitex平台，构建了电子词典，通过有限状态传感器形式化动词词干形态变化与屈折词缀组合，生成78个词干变体传感器和271个形态分析传感器，并优先考虑可读性以便语言学家扩展更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为马达加斯加语简单动词构建一个基于词典的形态分析器，填补该语言在自动形态分析方面的空白。

Method: 使用Unitex平台，构建电子词典，将动词词干形态变化及其与屈折词缀的组合形式化为有限状态传感器（FST），以可编辑图形表示。78个传感器生成词干变体词典，271个传感器用于识别已变形动词中的词干和词缀。

Result: 构建了78个生成词干变体的传感器和271个用于形态分析的传感器，实现了对马达加斯加语简单动词的基于词典的形态分析。

Conclusion: 所设计的词典和传感器优先考虑可读性，便于语言学家扩展和更新，为马达加斯加语的自然语言处理提供了基础资源。

Abstract: This paper reports the core linguistic work performed to construct a dictionary-based morphological analyser for Malagasy simple verbs. It uses the Unitex platform and comprised the contruction of an electronic dictionary for Malagasy simple verbs. The data is encoded on the basis of morphological features. The morphological variations of verb stems and their combination with inflectional affixes are formalized in finite-state transducers represented by editable graphs. 78 transducers allow Unitex to generate a dictionary of allomorphs of stems. 271 other transducers are used by the morphological analyser of Unitex to recognize the stem and the affixes in conjugated verbs. The design of the dictionary and transducers prioritizes readability, so that they can be extended and updated by linguists.

</details>


### [77] [Grounding Text Embeddings in Stakeholder Associations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27168)
*Jonathan Rystrøm,Sofie Burgos-Thorsen,Zihao Fu,Johan Irving Søltoft,Kenneth C. Enevoldsen,Chris Russell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出利益相关者扎根练习（Stakeholder Grounding Exercise），用于评估文本嵌入模型是否捕捉到对人类专家有意义的语义区分。以丹麦政策议题为主要案例，发现神经文本嵌入的可靠性显著低于人类专家（差距19-26个百分点），且这种偏差会传播到下游聚类任务。美国联邦AI用例的复制研究（差距16个百分点）表明该问题并非单一工具或领域的特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本嵌入被广泛用于分析大规模复杂文本语料，但嵌入模型捕捉的语义距离是否与使用它们的人类专家一致尚不明确。确保嵌入表示与人类意图对齐是进行有效分析的必要前提。

Method: 提出利益相关者扎根练习（Stakeholder Grounding Exercise），一种将专家关联性显式化并使嵌入模型结果根植于人类理解的方法。通过专家评估与模型结果对比，量化嵌入与人类判断之间的对齐程度。

Result: 在丹麦政策议题案例中，神经文本嵌入可靠性比人类专家低19-26个百分点；这种不对齐会传播到下游聚类性能（练习排名与聚类质量之间的Spearman ρ=0.9）。美国联邦AI用例复制研究在英语环境下使用数字协议和不同专家群体，复现了16个百分点的差距。

Conclusion: 利益相关者扎根练习提供了评估嵌入模型是否捕捉到对领域专家最重要的语义区分的实用方法。嵌入与人类判断之间的不对齐是跨领域、跨语言的普遍问题，需要引起重视。

Abstract: Text embeddings are widely used to analyse large corpora of complex texts. However, it is unclear whether the embeddings capture the same semantic distances as the human experts using them. Ensuring alignment between embedding representations and human intentions is essential for valid analyses. We present the Stakeholder Grounding Exercise, a method for making expert associations explicit and grounding embedding model results in human understanding. In our primary case study on Danish policy issues, we find that neural text embeddings are substantially less reliable than human experts (19-26 pp gap), and that this misalignment propagates to downstream clustering performance (Spearman $ρ=0.9$ between exercise ranking and cluster quality). A secondary study on US Federal AI use cases replicates the gap (16pp) in English, using a digital protocol and a different community of experts -- demonstrating that the gap is not an artefact of a single instrument or domain. The Stakeholder Grounding Exercise offers a practical method for assessing whether embedding models capture the semantic distinctions that matter most to domain experts.

</details>


### [78] [MAIGO: Mitigating Lost-in-Conversation with History-Cleaned On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27186)
*Haoyu Zheng,Yun Zhu,Shu Yuan,Shangming Chen,Qing Wang,Wenqiao Zhang,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型在多轮对话中会出现"迷失在对话中"(LiC)的性能下降问题，本文将其部分归因于"自污染"机制——中间助手回复进入后续上下文导致早期偏差被向前传播。为此提出MAIGO方法，一种基于策略的自蒸馏方法，利用模型自身策略生成的历史清理参考来减少污染，无需验证器奖励、状态标签或推理时辅助。实验表明该方法显著提升了多轮对话性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在完整指定提示的任务上表现良好，但在相同需求跨多轮展开时性能下降（LiC gap），作者将部分退化归因于自污染机制（中间助手回复污染后续上下文），旨在通过训练手段减轻该问题。

Method: 提出MAIGO，一种on-policy自蒸馏方法：(1) 中间轮次：移除先前助手回复，保留用户可见的分片前缀作为清理参考；(2) 答案轮次：从基于完整用户侧对话的配对全视图参考中蒸馏；(3) 使用可靠性权重降低与清理参考不一致的中间轮次样本权重。无需验证器奖励、状态标签或推理时辅助。

Result: 在LiC配对视图协议下，Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct的SHARDED准确率从52.8提升至66.1，SHARDED/FULL比率从66.5%提升至84.1%，而FULL准确率仅下降2.3个点。

Conclusion: 自污染是LiC gap中一个可通过训练解决的组成部分，MAIGO方法有效缓解了该问题。

Abstract: Large language models often solve tasks from a fully specified prompt but degrade when the same requirements unfold over multiple turns, known as the lost-in-conversation (LiC) gap. We trace part of this degradation to self-contamination: intermediate assistant replies enter later context and carry early deviations forward. Motivated by this mechanism, we propose MAIGO, an on-policy self-distillation method that reduces this contamination using history-cleaned references from the model's own policy. For middle turns, MAIGO removes prior assistant replies while preserving the user-visible sharded prefix; for answer turns, it distills from paired full-view references conditioned on the completed user-side dialogue. A reliability weight downweights middle-turn samples that disagree with the clean reference. MAIGO requires no verifier rewards, state labels, or inference-time scaffolding. Under the LiC paired-view protocol with deterministic verifiers, MAIGO improves Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct SHARDED accuracy from 52.8 to 66.1 and the SHARDED/FULL ratio from 66.5% to 84.1%, while keeping FULL accuracy within 2.3 points. These results show that self-contamination is a trainable component of the LiC gap.

</details>


### [79] [Beyond Binary: Speech Representations Across the Cognitive Score Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27189)
*Serli Kopar,Roshan Prakash Rane,Christian Mychajliw,Lydia Federmann,Gerhard Eschweiler,Daniela Berg,Sam Gijsen,Paula Andrea Perez-Toro,Kerstin Ritter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究利用5,754份德语神经心理学评估录音，分析了轻度认知障碍中语音表征与认知评估层级结构的关系。研究发现：自监督学习嵌入在低层级任务中优于手工声学特征，但在MCI分类中趋势逆转；任务约束影响层级性能——高自由度的任务呈现"专才"表征（高层级性能稀释），而高结构化任务呈现"通才"表征（高层级性能提升）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语音表征与认知评估层级结构（任务级、领域级、全局级）之间的关系，特别是不同语音特征（手工声学特征 vs. 自监督学习嵌入）在不同层级上的表现差异，以及任务约束如何影响层级性能变化。

Method: 使用5,754份德语神经心理学评估录音，涵盖六项认知任务，在三个分数层级（任务级、领域级、全局级）上进行评估，比较手工声学特征与自监督学习（SSL）嵌入的表现。

Result: 自监督学习表示在较低层级普遍优于手工特征，但在MCI分类中趋势逆转；任务约束影响层级性能：高自由度任务随层级升高性能稀释（专才表征），高结构化任务随层级升高性能提升（通才表征）。

Conclusion: 任务约束与评估层级之间存在系统性关联，揭示了自动化临床语音分析中任务约束与评估层级之间的联系。

Abstract: This study examines the relationship between speech representations and the hierarchical structure of cognitive assessment in mild cognitive impairment. Utilizing 5,754 German neuropsychological assessment recordings, we evaluate six cognitive tasks across three score levels: task, domain, and global levels. We compare hand-crafted acoustic features with self-supervised learning (SSL) embeddings. Results show that although SSL representations generally outperform hand-crafted features at lower levels, this trend reverses for MCI classification. Furthermore, task-specific constraints influence performance: tasks with greater response freedom exhibit performance dilution as hierarchical levels increase, suggesting ``specialist'' representations, whereas the performance of highly structured tasks increases toward higher levels, suggesting ``generalist'' representations. These findings show links between task constraints and assessment hierarchy in automated clinical speech analysis.

</details>


### [80] [Learning When to Think While Listening in Large Audio-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27190)
*Zhiyuan Song,Weici Zhao,Yang Xiao,Suhao Yu,Cheng Zhu,Jiatao Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于大型音频语言模型（LALM）的可学习"等待-思考-回答"控制框架。在流式语音交互中，控制器根据部分音频证据动态决策何时等待、何时输出紧凑推理更新、何时给出最终答案。基于Qwen2.5-Omni-7B构建对齐轨迹，采用SFT+DAPO多奖励优化。在六任务合成SRQA基准上，六奖励DAPO将准确率从67.6%提升至70.3%，同时后端点最终思考长度降低14%。在186项真实语音基准上，SFT准确率最高，六奖励DAPO是唯一最终思考长度低于基线的学习变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式语音交互中推理质量与响应时间存在根本矛盾：过早回答可能证据不足，过晚回答则导致用户感知延迟。受人类对话增量式特点启发，需要让模型学会在音频流进行中动态决策何时等待、何时输出中间推理、何时给出最终答案。

Method: 1. 基于Qwen2.5-Omni-7B构建对齐的等待-思考-回答轨迹；2. 采用监督微调（SFT）训练控制器；3. 应用解耦裁剪与动态采样策略优化（DAPO），使用六项奖励函数（答案正确性、动作有效性、更新时机、延迟同步、推理质量、链一致性）优化完整轨迹而非仅最终答案。

Result: 在六任务合成SRQA基准上，六奖励DAPO控制器将行加权准确率从67.6%提升至70.3%，后端点最终思考长度降低14%。在186项真实语音基准上，所有控制器变体均有效，SFT准确率最强，六奖励DAPO是唯一最终思考长度低于基线的学习变体。

Conclusion: 流式语音模型应学习在音频流中明确何时输出中间推理。可学习的等待-思考-回答控制策略能够在不牺牲准确率的前提下有效降低响应延迟，表明动态中间推理外化是流式交互的关键能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Audio-Language Models (LALMs) have made real-time, streaming spoken interaction increasingly practical. In this setting, reasoning quality and responsiveness are tightly coupled: delaying reasoning until the speech endpoint can improve answer quality but moves deliberation into user-visible response delay, while answering too early risks committing before decisive evidence arrives. We introduce a learnable wait-think-answer control formulation for LALMs. Motivated by the incremental nature of human conversation, the controller decides under partial audio evidence when to wait, when to externalize a compact reasoning update, and when to answer. Using Qwen2.5-Omni-7B as the base model, we construct aligned wait-think-answer traces from spoken reasoning data, train the controller with supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and then apply Decoupled Clip and Dynamic Sampling Policy Optimization (DAPO). The reward combines answer correctness, action validity, update timing, latency synchronization, reasoning quality, and chain consistency, optimizing the complete wait-think-answer trajectory and not the final answer alone. On a six-task synthetic spoken reasoning question answering (SRQA) benchmark, the six-reward DAPO controller improves the row-weighted accuracy from 67.6% to 70.3% while reducing post-endpoint final-think length by 14% under the same Qwen deployment harness. On a 186-item human-recorded Real Audio Bench, a transfer check beyond text-to-speech (TTS)-rendered speech, the controller family remains functional: SFT achieves the strongest accuracy, while the six-reward DAPO controller is the only learned variant whose final-think length falls below the base. These results suggest that a streaming model should learn when to make intermediate reasoning explicit during the audio stream.

</details>


### [81] [EpiCurveBench: Evaluating VLMs on Epidemic Curve Digitization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27195)
*Thomas Berkane,Maimuna S. Majumder*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对现有图表数据提取基准（如ChartQA）性能趋于饱和、评估指标忽略时间序列结构的缺陷，本文提出EpiCurveBench基准（1000张真实流行病曲线图）和EpiCurveSimilarity（ECS）评估指标（基于动态规划对齐序列，容忍并惩罚局部偏移和空缺）。实验表明最强模型仅达52.3% ECS，ECS能将通用VLM的差距从KV指标的5分位扩展至25分位，且ECS与四项下游流行病学统计量相关性比DTW强1.5-3.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表数据提取基准（如ChartQA）上前沿VLM已超89%准确率，性能提升空间有限；且评估指标将提取点视为无序键值对，忽略了时间序列的时序结构，对微小对齐偏移施以灾难性惩罚，无法合理评估真实场景中的图表提取质量。

Method: 构建EpiCurveBench基准（1000张真实世界流行病曲线图，来自多样公共卫生数据源）；提出EpiCurveSimilarity（ECS）评估指标，通过动态规划对齐预测序列与真值序列，容忍并按比例惩罚局部时间偏移和空缺。评估六种方法（三种前沿闭源VLM、一种开源VLM、两种专用图表提取系统）。

Result: 最强模型仅达52.3% ECS；通用VLM在ECS上分布于25分位范围，而RMS/SCRM等键值指标将其压缩至5分位范围。ECS与四项下游流行病学汇总统计量（总计数、峰值时间、峰值幅度、增长率保真度）相关性比DTW强1.5-3.6倍。

Conclusion: EpiCurveBench和ECS有效揭示了现有VLM在时间序列图表提取上的实际性能差距，为高影响力的公共卫生应用（解锁被困在已发表图表中的数十年疫情数据）提供了更合理的评估工具，且该方法可直接适用于任何结构化时间序列图表提取场景。

Abstract: Chart-to-data extraction with vision-language models (VLMs) is increasingly evaluated on benchmarks that show diminishing headroom (frontier VLMs exceed 89% on ChartQA) and with metrics that treat extracted points as unordered key-value pairs, ignoring the temporal structure of time series and penalizing small alignment shifts as catastrophic failures. We address both gaps with EpiCurveBench, a benchmark of 1,000 real-world epidemic curve images curated from diverse public-health sources, and EpiCurveSimilarity (ECS), an evaluation metric that aligns predicted and ground-truth series via dynamic programming, tolerating local temporal shifts and gaps while penalizing them proportionally. Evaluating six methods--three frontier closed VLMs, one open VLM, and two specialized chart-extraction systems--we find the strongest model reaches only 52.3% ECS, and that ECS spreads the four general-purpose VLMs over a 25-point range where key-value metrics (RMS, SCRM) compress them into a 5-point band. We further validate ECS against four downstream epidemiological summary statistics, finding that higher ECS predicts smaller errors in total counts, peak timing, and peak magnitude, and higher growth-rate fidelity; across all four, ECS correlates 1.5--3.6 times more strongly than Dynamic Time Warping, which lacks a gap penalty and therefore cannot distinguish a truncated prediction from a temporally faithful one. EpiCurveBench targets a high-impact public-health application--unlocking decades of outbreak data trapped in published figures--but the benchmark and metric apply directly to any structured time-series chart-extraction setting.

</details>


### [82] [Temporal Simultaneity Predicts Annotation Quality in Sentiment Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27239)
*Idris Abdulmumin,Mokgadi Penelope Matloga,Tadesse Destaw Belay,Botshelo Kondowe,Letlhogonolo Mohleleng,Hareaipha Nkopo Letsoalo,Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad,Vukosi Marivate*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对Setswana语情感标注数据集（3,565条推文）中随时间推移标注质量下降的问题展开研究，发现尽管总体Kappa值达0.76（优秀），但逐批Kappa下降超32点。通过六项分析揭示：标签混淆集中在负/中性边界、两名标注者出现自动驾驶式漂移、时间同步性是Kappa主导预测因子（同1分钟内标注κ=0.98，超1天仅0.65）。微调多语言编码器及GPT-5/Gemini模型后，GPT-5少样本学习达62.2 macro-F1最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当标注活动跨越数周或数月且标注者池较小时，标注质量难以维持。现有研究缺乏对非洲低资源语言标注过程中标注者一致性随时间衰减的系统性分析。

Method: 构建含3,565条Setswana推文的三人标注数据集，分8批次进行；采用Randolph's free-marginal Kappa评估一致性；通过六项针对性分析（标签混淆矩阵、标注者漂移、时间同步性、标注速度、语言特征等）探究IAA下降原因；并对比三种开源多语言编码器与GPT-5/Gemini的情感分类性能。

Result: 总体κ=0.76但逐批下降超32点；负/中性边界混淆最严重；两名标注者出现自动驾驶式漂移；时间同步性是最强预测因子（同1分钟内κ=0.98，超1天仅0.65）；标注速度与语言特征与κ无显著关联；微调提升29-43 macro-F1，GPT-5少样本达62.2 macro-F1最优。

Conclusion: 时间不同步是标注质量下降的主因而非语言复杂性；建议标注时保持密集同步以避免质量衰减；开源数据集、时间戳和分析代码以支持非洲语言NLP资源的可复现质量审计。

Abstract: Annotation quality is difficult to sustain when campaigns span weeks or months with small annotator pools. We present a Setswana sentiment dataset of 3,565 tweets annotated by three native-speaker annotators across eight batches and examine why inter-annotator agreement (IAA) declines over time. Despite an aggregate Randolph's free-marginal Kappa of $κ= 0.76$, "excellent," per-batch $κ$ falls by more than 32 points across the annotation task. Through six targeted analyses, we find that (i) label confusion concentrates on the negative/neutral boundary, (ii) two annotators show run-length drift consistent with autopilot labeling, and (iii) the dominant predictor of $κ$ is temporal simultaneity: tweets labeled within one minute achieve $κ= 0.98$, while those labeled more than a day apart reach only $κ= 0.65$. Annotation speed and tweet-level linguistic features show no meaningful association with $κ$. We benchmark three open multilingual encoders and proprietary models (GPT-5 and Gemini) on three-class sentiment classification; fine-tuning yields gains of 29 to 43 macro-F1 points over pretrained baselines, with GPT-5 few-shot leading overall (62.2 macro-F1). We release the dataset, per-annotation timestamps, and analysis code to support reproducible quality auditing for future African language NLP resources.

</details>


### [83] [Pair-In, Pair-Out: Latent Multi-Token Prediction for Efficient LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27255)
*Wenhui Tan,Minghao Li,Xiaoqian Ma,Siqi Fan,Xiusheng Huang,Liujie Zhang,Ruihua Song,Weihang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Pair-In, Pair-Out (PIPO)框架，统一输入侧（隐层压缩）和输出侧（多token预测）的推理加速方法。通过将压缩器与MTP头视为镜像操作，并训练轻量级置信度头替代昂贵的验证器，结合在线策略蒸馏实现无额外开销的可靠草稿token接受。在多个基准上取得最高2.64倍首token延迟和2.07倍每token延迟加速，pass@4提升最多7.15个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将输入侧隐层压缩和输出侧推测解码/MTP独立研究，且输出侧方法需要昂贵的验证器来校验不可靠的草稿token，缺乏统一框架且效率低下。

Method: 将压缩器（折叠两个输入token为一个隐表示）与MTP头（展开一个隐状态为一个额外输出token）视为镜像操作统一框架；训练轻量级置信度头决定草稿token是否被接受，利用在线策略蒸馏自然匹配推测解码的拒绝采样准则，无需额外验证器开销。

Result: 在AIME 2025、GPQA-Diamond、LiveCodeBench v6、LongBench v2上，使用Qwen3.5-4B和9B骨干，pass@4提升最多7.15个点，首token延迟加速最高2.64倍，每token延迟加速最高2.07倍。

Conclusion: PIPO成功统一了输入输出侧的推理加速方法，以轻量级置信度头替代昂贵验证器，实现了高效可靠的推理加速。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought reasoning has made autoregressive decoding the dominant inference cost of modern large language models. Existing methods target either the input side (latent compression) or the output side (speculative decoding and multi-token prediction, MTP), but the two lines of work have been pursued independently. Moreover, output-side methods must incur an expensive verifier pass to validate the unreliable draft tokens predicted by MTP. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{Pair-In, Pair-Out (PIPO)}, which unifies both sides by viewing a latent compressor and an MTP head as mirror-image operations: the compressor folds two input tokens into one latent representation, while the MTP head unfolds one hidden state into one additional output token. To remove the verifier cost without sacrificing reliability, PIPO trains a lightweight confidence head that decides whether draft tokens should be accepted. We observe that On-Policy Distillation (OPD) naturally matches the rejection-sampling criterion of speculative decoding, so the confidence head can be trained alongside OPD with negligible extra cost. Experiments on AIME 2025, GPQA-Diamond, LiveCodeBench v6, and LongBench v2 with Qwen3.5-4B and 9B backbones show that PIPO improves pass@4 over regular decoding by up to $+7.15$ points, while delivering up to $2.64\times$ first-token-latency and $2.07\times$ per-token-latency speedups.

</details>


### [84] [Lost in Sampling: Assessing Lexical Reachability in LLMs via the Word Coverage Score (WCS)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27268)
*Samer Awad,Javier Conde,Carlos Arriaga,Tairan Fu,Javier Coronado-Blázquez,Pedro Reviriego*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出词覆盖率得分（WCS）来量化标准采样过滤器（Top-p、Top-k、Min-p）如何在不经意间抑制低频率、高信息量的人类词汇，导致LLM生成文本的词汇多样性降低。实验证明行业默认采样参数充当了非预期的审查机制，将人类表达的独特纹理平滑为同质化话语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM拥有巨大的潜在词汇量，但生成的文本却常常重复且同质化。现有研究大多关注模型知识和训练数据，本文则从解码机制入手，探究采样过滤器对语言多样性的抑制作用。

Method: 提出词覆盖率得分（Word Coverage Score, WCS），通过衡量标准采样过滤器（Top-p、Top-k、Min-p）在数学上剪除上下文恰当的人类词汇的程度，计算低频率、高信息量人类词汇作为采样参数函数的词法存活率。在人类作者语料片段上审计开源模型，识别哪些合理的词汇选择被解码器排除。

Result: 定量证据表明，行业标准的采样默认值充当了非预期的审查机制，将人类表达的独特纹理平滑为同质化话语。

Conclusion: WCS提供了一个用于优化文本连贯性与词汇丰富度之间权衡的严谨框架，可作为在生成模型中保留人类语言多样性的诊断工具。

Abstract: Modern Large Language Models (LLMs) are often criticized for producing repetitive and homogeneous text, despite possessing vast latent vocabularies. While previous research has focused on model knowledge and training data, we investigate the role of decoding mechanics in suppressing linguistic diversity. We introduce the Word Coverage Score (WCS), a metric that quantifies the extent to which contextually appropriate human vocabulary is mathematically pruned by standard sampling filters (e.g., Top-$p$, Top-$k$, and Min-$p$). Rather than assessing static knowledge, the WCS measures the lexical survival rate of low-frequency, high-information human words as a function of sampling parameters. By auditing open-weight models on human-authored corpus fragments, we identify which logical lexical choices are rendered unreachable by the decoder, even when they reside within the probability space. Our results provide quantitative evidence that industry-standard sampling defaults act as unintended censorship mechanisms, smoothing the unique textures of human expression into a homogenized discourse. The WCS offers a rigorous framework for optimizing the trade-off between text coherence and lexical richness, providing a diagnostic tool for preserving the diversity of human language in generative models.

</details>


### [85] [It's Not Always Sycophancy: Measuring LLM Conformity as a Function of Epistemic Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27288)
*Kevin H. Guo,Chao Yan,Avinash Baidya,Katherine Brown,Xiang Gao,Juming Xiong,Zhijun Yin,Bradley A. Malin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MUSE框架，揭示大语言模型在对话中顺从用户反驳的行为不仅源于谄媚（sycophancy），还受模型推理时的认知不确定性（epistemic uncertainty）驱动。两类顺从行为均随用户感知专业度和建议合理性增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将LLM放弃初始立场顺从用户的行为归因于RLHF训练导致的谄媚，但作者假设顺从性还与模型在推理时的认知不确定性有关，需要系统解耦两种机制。

Method: 提出MUSE两阶段评估框架：首先映射模型对查询的认知不确定性，再评估其在后续轮次中对用户反驳的顺从倾向，从而区分谄媚驱动与不确定性驱动的顺从行为。

Result: 1) 顺从行为由两种机制共同驱动：谄媚性顺从（即使模型对初始回答绝对确定仍顺从用户）和不确定性驱动的顺从（不确定性越高，顺从倾向越大）；2) 两种顺从均随用户感知专业度和建议合理性增强而增长。

Conclusion: MUSE可用于制定更有针对性的干预策略，通过区分对齐阶段引入的谄媚与训练语料导致的不确定性来缓解模型过度顺从问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are known to abandon their initial stance to conform to user pushback. While prior research largely attributes this behavior to sycophancy learned during reinforcement learning from human feedback, we hypothesize that conformity is also driven by a model's epistemic uncertainty at inference time. In this paper, we introduce MUSE, a two-stage evaluation framework to disentangle the mechanisms driving LLM conformity. Specifically, MUSE maps a model's epistemic uncertainty in responding to a query against its likelihood to yield to user pushback in a subsequent turn. We demonstrate that the mechanisms driving conformity extend beyond sycophancy alone. Specifically, we characterize two distinct factors that jointly drive conformity: sycophantic conformity, where a model aligns with user pushback even with absolute certainty in its initial response, and uncertainty-driven conformity, where a model's likelihood for conformity increases alongside its uncertainty. Furthermore, we conduct ablation studies to demonstrate that both sycophantic conformity and uncertainty-driven conformity grow with 1) the LLM's perceived expertise of the user and 2) the plausibility of the user's suggestions. More broadly, MUSE informs more targeted intervention strategies by distinguishing alignment-induced sycophancy and training-corpora-driven uncertainty.

</details>


### [86] [Probing Cultural Awareness in LLMs: A Case Study of Cross-Culture Aesthetic Stylistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27296)
*Jiashuo Wang,Fenggang Yu,Jian Wang,Chak Tou Leong,Xiaoyu Shen,Chunpu Xu,Jiawen Duan,Wenjie Li,Johan F. Hoorn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了大语言模型在不同文化背景下对美学风格的掌握能力，通过构建C4STYLI基准（包含香港和中国大陆高度风格化的翻译电影片名和广告标语），从行为识别和生成能力两个维度进行评估。发现LLM在风格识别上与人类存在差异，且识别与生成能力不一致。通过结构消融实验发现，LLM对香港风格的识别主要依赖表层语言信息而非风格结构，表明其对香港特有风格结构的敏感性有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地部署在不同文化背景中，但它们掌握美学风格（即通过语言策略性运用来唤起文化共鸣的能力）仍然未被充分探索。

Method: 构建C4STYLI基准（包含高度风格化的翻译电影片名和广告标语），通过行为识别和生成能力两个维度评估LLM；结合逻辑回归探针进行结构消融实验。

Result: LLM在风格识别上与人类存在差异，且识别能力随文本领域变化而不同；风格识别与生成能力不一致；在香港场景中，LLM的风格识别主要依赖表层语言信息而非风格结构。

Conclusion: LLM对香港特有风格结构的敏感性有限，其风格识别更多基于浅层语言特征而非深层的风格结构信息。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in diverse cultural contexts, yet their ability to master aesthetic stylistics, i.e., the strategic use of language to evoke cultural resonance, remains underexplored. We curate C4STYLI, a benchmark of highly stylized translated movie titles and advertising slogans from Hong Kong and the Chinese Mainland, to evaluate LLMs via the lens of behavioral recognition and productive competence. Extensive evaluations show that LLMs differ from humans in stylistic recognition, and this recognition ability varies across text domains. In addition, stylistic recognition and generation performance in LLMs are not consistently aligned. To further examine whether LLMs genuinely capture stylistic information in stylistic recognition, we conduct structural ablation with logistic regression probes. We find that, in the Hong Kong setting, stylistic recognition in LLMs relies primarily on surface-level linguistic information rather than stylistic structure. This suggests limited sensitivity to Hong Kong-specific stylistic structure.

</details>


### [87] [Self-Ensembling Vision-Language Models for Chart Data Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27298)
*Thomas Berkane,Qianyi Wang,Maimuna S. Majumder*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种VLM自集成方法，通过多次采样并聚合表格输出，有效提升了图表到表格的提取精度，尤其在复杂图表上取得显著改进，并引入新基准WB-ChartExtract。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图表中的定量信息常以图像形式存在，难以直接复用和分析；现有VLM方法在数据点密集或风格多样的图表上表现不佳，亟需更鲁棒的提取方法。

Method: 对同一图表图像从同一VLM多次采样生成多个候选表格，对齐后对每个数值单元格取中位数聚合得到共识表格；引入收敛检测以自适应停止采样，并基于样本离散度提供不确定性估计。

Result: 在ChartQA和WB-ChartExtract两个基准上，该方法相比单次VLM输出提升了提取精度，在WB-ChartExtract上相对提升达23%。

Conclusion: 该自集成方法有效解锁了图表图像中的表格数据，支持下游分析与复用；新基准WB-ChartExtract为复杂图表提取提供了更具挑战性的评估平台。

Abstract: Charts effectively convey quantitative information, but the underlying data are often locked in image form, hindering reuse and analysis. Manually digitizing charts is time-consuming and error-prone, motivating automatic chart-to-table extraction. Recent approaches use specialized vision-language models (VLMs), yet performance still lags on charts with many datapoints or substantial stylistic variation. We propose a VLM self-ensembling method that repeatedly samples multiple tabular outputs from the same VLM for a fixed chart image and aggregates them at the level of individual table cells. We align candidate tables and take per-cell medians over numerical values to produce a more accurate consensus table. Our method also includes convergence detection to stop sampling once the aggregated table stabilizes, and uncertainty estimation based on dispersion across samples to help users assess extraction reliability. Because existing chart extraction benchmarks contain relatively simple plots with limited room for improvement, we introduce WB-ChartExtract, a new benchmark built from World Bank data with more complex and stylistically diverse charts; on average, its charts contain 7 times more datapoints than those in the ChartQA benchmark. Across both ChartQA and WB-ChartExtract, our approach improves extraction accuracy over single-pass VLM outputs, yielding up to 23% relative improvement on WB-ChartExtract after ensembling. More broadly, our method helps unlock tabular data previously siloed in chart images, enabling downstream analysis and reuse.

</details>


### [88] [Chartographer: Counterfactual Chart Generation for Evaluating Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27311)
*Yifan Jiang,Dae Yon Hwang,Jesse C. Cresswell,Freda Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出反事实图表框架Chartographer，通过逆向工程将图表转为可执行代码，生成受控变体来严格评估VLM的视觉推理能力，发现模型在原始图表正确的情况下仍难以泛化到反事实变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表QA基准中模型可通过捷径或背景知识绕开视觉推理来作答，无法严格评估真实的视觉推理能力。

Method: 提出Chartographer框架：将图表逆向工程为可执行代码，验证重建保真度，生成种子控制的反事实变体，从可执行QA逻辑中推导新答案。

Result: 反事实图表揭示了单图表评估隐藏的失败：VLM在正确回答原始图表后仍难以泛化，尤其在需要新视觉推理路径时失败最为普遍。

Conclusion: 现有VLM在图表视觉推理任务中存在显著的泛化缺陷，反事实评估能有效暴露这些隐蔽的失败模式。

Abstract: Chart question-answering (QA) benchmarks aim to pose questions that require visual reasoning to correctly answer, but models can often reach solutions through shortcuts or prior familiarity with a chart based on their own background knowledge. To strictly evaluate visual reasoning, we propose counterfactual charts where the chart-question task remains fixed, but underlying chart and the corresponding answer are varied. We introduce Chartographer, a framework to reverse engineer charts into executable code, validate reconstruction fidelity, generate seed-controlled counterfactual variants, and derive new answers from executable QA logic. We apply this framework to existing chart QA datasets and evaluate proprietary and open-source vision-language models (VLMs), measuring variation sensitivity and generalizability. Counterfactual charts reveal failures hidden by single-chart performance: VLMs often fail to generalize after answering the original chart correctly. We find failures are most prevalent when updated charts require novel visual reasoning pathways.

</details>


### [89] [When Does Demographic Information Help? Data and Modeling Regimes for Perspective-Aware Hate Speech Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27313)
*Weibin Cai,Reza Zafarani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了人口统计信息在主观标注任务（如仇恨言论检测）中有助于提升性能的条件，发现其效果取决于数据划分特性（标注者分歧程度、训练规模、人口覆盖重叠）和建模框架，并提出了一种门控残差模型来选择性利用人口统计信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人口统计信息在主观任务标注建模中的效果不稳定——有时提升性能，有时沦为噪声。现有研究缺乏对其有效条件的系统性分析，本文旨在回答"人口统计特征何时有帮助"这一核心问题。

Method: 1) 将人口统计增益分析为数据划分特性（标注者分歧度、训练规模、训练-测试人口覆盖重叠）和建模框架的函数；2) 提出门控人口残差模型（Gated Demographic Residual Model），将人口统计信息作为文本预测的选择性调整项，仅在需要时激活。

Result: 人口统计增益集中在以下场景：低训练分歧、高测试分歧、细粒度歧义度量、充足训练数据、较大的人口重叠度。提出的门控残差模型在MHS和POPQUORN数据集上效果显著，尤其在高分歧或低置信度样本上表现突出。

Conclusion: 人口统计信息不应默认有用，其价值共同取决于数据场景和建模框架。研究者需要根据具体条件审慎决定是否引入人口统计特征。

Abstract: Demographic information is often used to model annotator perspectives in subjective tasks such as hate speech detection, but its benefit is inconsistent: it improves performance in some settings and behaves as noise in others. This paper asks when demographic features help. We analyze demographic gain as a function of both data split properties and modeling frameworks. For data splits, we measure annotator disagreement, namely how often annotators assign different labels to the same example, along with training size and train-test demographic coverage. We find that demographic gains concentrate in regimes with low training disagreement, high test disagreement, fine-grained ambiguity measurement, sufficient training data, and greater demographic overlap. Motivated by these regimes, we introduce a gated demographic residual model that treats demographics as a selective adjustment to text-only predictions. Experiments on MHS and POPQUORN show that this design is effective, especially on high disagreement or low confidence examples. Overall, our results suggest that demographics should not be assumed useful by default; their value depends jointly on the data regime and the modeling framework.

</details>


### [90] [Real Images, Worse Judgments: Evaluating Vision-Language Models on Concreteness and Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27315)
*Yifan Jiang,Ruoxi Ning,Sheng Yao,Freda Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文质疑多模态模型中视觉输入总能提升语言理解的假设。通过人类具体性和意象评级实验，发现真实图像上下文不仅不能一致提升VLM与人类判断的对齐度，反而在视觉证据最不相关时损害最大。分析表明真实图像导致表征偏移和对虚假视觉线索的敏感度增加，而推理时指导模型仅关注文本可缓解此退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验"视觉输入总能改善语言理解"这一常见假设是否成立，探究视觉-语言模型能否区分有用的视觉证据与无关的图像上下文。

Method: 使用人类具体性和意象评级作为基准，对比有无真实图像上下文下VLM的表现；通过探针分析、典型相关分析和归因案例研究分析表征变化；测试推理时指令引导仅关注文本的效果。

Result: 真实图像上下文不带来一致提升，在视觉证据最不相关时损害最大；真实图像导致表征偏移和对虚假视觉线索的敏感度增加，削弱目标词汇属性的可恢复性；推理时指导模型仅关注文本可减少退化，在脆弱子集上效果最明显。

Conclusion: 当前指令微调的VLM需要更好地校准何时视觉上下文应参与词汇判断，视觉输入并非总是有益。

Abstract: Visual inputs are often assumed to improve language understanding in multimodal models. We examine this assumption by asking whether vision-language models (VLMs) can distinguish useful visual evidence from incidental image context in lexical judgments. We use human concreteness and imagery ratings because they span words with varying expected visual relevance, from abstract and low-imagery words to concrete and high-imagery words. We find that real-image contexts do not yield consistent gains and often hurt alignment with human ratings, most sharply when visual evidence is least relevant. Through probing and canonical correlation analysis, complemented by an attribution case study, we find that real-image contexts are associated with representational shifts and greater sensitivity to spurious visual cues, coinciding with weaker recoverability of the targeted lexical properties. We further show that instructing models to focus solely on textual content at inference time can reduce this degradation, with the clearest gains on these vulnerable subsets. Our findings suggest that current instruction-tuned VLMs need better calibration of when visual context should inform lexical judgments.

</details>


### [91] [Semantic Gradients Interactions in SSD: A Case Study in Racial Identity and Hate Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27322)
*Felix Ostrowicki,Hubert Plisiecki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Interaction SSD，一种扩展的监督语义差异方法，用于建模语义意义在不同调节变量（如群体、特质或条件）下的变化，并通过仇恨言论语料库验证了该方法能够检测和解释调节效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SSD方法无法检验语义意义在不同群体或条件下的变化，缺乏对调节效应的统计检验能力。

Method: 在SSD基础上引入交互项，估计主语义梯度、交互梯度和条件梯度，所有梯度均可通过标准SSD工具解释。

Result: 在UC Berkeley仇恨言论语料库上，模型检测到显著的调节效应：共享梯度区分非人性化敌意与反言论，交互梯度揭示群体间在语义线索预测仇恨评分上的较小差异。

Conclusion: Interaction SSD使调节性的意义-结果关系在统计上可检验且可解释。

Abstract: We introduce interaction SSD, an extension of Supervised Semantic Differential that models how semantic meaning varies across moderators such as groups, traits, or conditions making this variation testable and interpretable. The method estimates a main semantic gradient, an interaction gradient, and conditional gradients, all interpretable through standard SSD tools. We illustrate it on the UC Berkeley Measuring Hate Speech corpus, testing whether annotator racial identity moderates hate-speech judgments of comments targeting people of color. The interaction model detects a significant moderation effect: the shared gradient contrasts dehumanizing hostility with counter-speech, while the interaction gradient reveals smaller group-linked differences in which semantic cues predict hate-speech ratings. Interaction SSD makes moderated meaning-outcome relationships statistically testable and interpretable.

</details>


### [92] [FinHarness: An Inline Lifecycle Safety Harness for Finance LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27333)
*Haoxuan Jia,Yang Liu,Bin Chong,Yingguang Yang,Yancheng Chen,Jiayu Liang,Qian Li,Hanning Lu,Kefu Xu,Hao Zheng,Chongyang Zhang,Hao Peng,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FinHarness 是一个为金融 LLM Agent 设计的端到端内联安全防护框架，通过查询监控、工具监控和级联裁决模块，将跨轮次意图漂移和工具调用风险实时注入 agent 输入，使其能够自主拒绝、重规划或批准。在 FinVault 基准上，攻击成功率从 38.3% 降至 15.0%，良性批准率仅从 41.1% 微降至 39.3%，且高级 judge 调用次数减少 4.7 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融 LLM Agent 的安全方案存在两个痛点：(1) 边界过滤器容易遗漏中间轨迹中不可逆的工具调用；(2) 事后 LLM 审计仅在终止后执行，无法干预且计算成本随轨迹长度线性增长。因此需要一个能在每一步进行实时干预且高效的安全机制。

Method: FinHarness 包含三个组件：(1) Query Monitor：融合单轮意图与跨轮次意图漂移；(2) Tool Monitor：评估每个即将执行的工具调用；(3) Cascade 模块：集成每步风险，自适应地在轻量级和高级 LLM Judge 之间路由验证。被触发的风险因子作为事前证据重新注入 agent 输入，使 agent 自行决定拒绝、重规划或批准。

Result: 在 FinVault 基准上，路由后的 FinHarness 将攻击成功率 (ASR) 从 38.3% 降至 15.0%，良性批准率从 41.1% 轻微下降至 39.3%，且相比始终使用高级 judge 的消融版本，高级 judge 调用次数减少了 4.7 倍。

Conclusion: FinHarness 通过内联实时监控和自适应级联验证，有效平衡了金融场景下 LLM Agent 的安全性与正常业务效率，显著降低攻击成功率的同时保持较高的良性任务通过率，并大幅节省计算资源。

Abstract: Finance LLM agents must simultaneously block prompt-induced unauthorized actions and approve legitimate multi-step business workflows. However, boundary filters often miss irreversible mid-trajectory tool calls, while post-hoc LLM judges perform auditing only after termination -- too late for intervention and at a computational cost that scales linearly with trace length. We present FinHarness, an inline safety harness that wraps a finance agent end-to-end with three components: a Query Monitor that fuses single-turn intent with cross-turn drift, a Tool Monitor that evaluates each prospective tool call, and a Cascade module that integrates per-step risk and adaptively routes verification between a lightweight and an advanced-tier LLM judge. Fired risk factors are re-injected into the agent input as ex-ante evidence, enabling the agent to refuse, re-plan, or approve on its own. On FinVault, routed FinHarness cuts ASR from 38.3% to 15.0% while largely preserving benign approval ($41.1\% \to 39.3\%$), and uses $4.7\times$ fewer advanced-judge calls than an always-advanced ablation.

</details>


### [93] [MATCHA: Matching Text via Contrastive Semantic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27345)
*Siran Li,Ece Sena Etoglu,Carsten Eickhoff,Seyed Ali Bahrainian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出现有自动评估指标（如ROUGE、BERTScore）无法有效区分语义矛盾，并提出MATCHA——一种同时奖励语义一致性和惩罚矛盾的双视角自动评估指标，在多个基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流评估指标（如ROUGE、BERTScore）在评估文本语义相似性时存在严重缺陷，经常给互相矛盾的文本分配几乎相同的分数，从而掩盖模型输出中的根本性错误。

Method: MATCHA采用双视角机制：一方面衡量生成文本与参考文本的语义接近程度，另一方面衡量其与对抗性生成的反事实矛盾的语义距离，从而同时奖励语义一致性和惩罚矛盾。

Result: 在八个公开基准上，MATCHA在QA、图像描述生成、NLI、摘要和语义文本相似性任务中均优于现有指标。在TruthfulQA上，相比ROUGE-L提升18.38%，相比BERTScore提升20.82%。与23个嵌入模型相比，MATCHA在仅基于参考文本区分正确与错误陈述方面最为准确。

Conclusion: MATCHA作为自动评估指标能更准确反映人类判断，揭示了现有指标的根本性缺陷，在无训练集场景下尤其有效。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation is essential for understanding large language model (LLM) performance, yet today's go-to metrics, namely token-overlap scores (e.g., ROUGE) and embedding-based measures (e.g., BERTScore), often misjudge semantic similarity of documents. Our study shows that both token-overlap metrics and embedding-based metrics routinely assign nearly identical scores to texts that directly contradict each other, thereby potentially masking fundamental errors. We introduce MATCHA, an automatic metric that jointly rewards semantic agreement with a reference and penalizes contradictions. MATCHA employs a dual-view perspective that measures (i) proximity to the gold text and (ii) distance from an adversarially generated counterfactual contradiction. In eight public benchmarks, MATCHA outperforms popular metrics, compared with human annotations on question-answering, image caption generation, natural language inference, summarization, and semantic textual similarity tasks. On the TruthfulQA dataset (i.e., a dataset without a training set, where no embedding-based metrics could locally train on), this improvement in terms of matching texts with a reference reaches 18.38% over ROUGE-L and 20.82% over BERTScore. Both quantitative comparison and qualitative human assessments confirm the efficacy and validity of MATCHA and uncover fundamental weaknesses in pre-existing metrics. Compared with 23 embedding models, including top state-of-the-art ones, used as a metric similar to BERTScore, MATCHA remains the most accurate in distinguishing correct from incorrect statements solely based on a reference. Our code and metric are publicly available (https://github.com/Siran-Li/MATCHA).

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [BrickAnything: Geometry-Conditioned Buildable Brick Generation with Structure-Aware Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26182)
*Zhengyang Ni,Feng Yan,Yu Guo,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BrickAnything是一个几何条件自回归框架，通过结构感知树分词、偏好对齐后训练、有效性约束解码和自适应回滚等技术，从多种3D表示生成可构建的砖块结构，在几何保真度和物理可实现性上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有砖块生成方法要么依赖启发式优化（目标形状无可行结构时会失败），要么生成砖块序列时未显式建模底层3D几何和装配关系，缺乏系统性解决方案。

Method: 采用点云作为统一几何接口；提出结构感知树分词（structure-aware tree tokenization）通过局部附着关系表示砖块结构；结合偏好对齐后训练、有效性约束解码和自适应回滚来优化可构建性目标（稳定性、几何保真度）。

Result: 该方法能生成几何保真且物理可实现的砖块结构；提出的树分词策略相比传统排序策略有效减少了回滚和重新生成次数。

Conclusion: BrickAnything在多种3D表示上实现了可构建砖块结构的可靠生成，其核心创新在于结构感知的序列化方式和多种有效性保障机制的协同设计。

Abstract: Generating physically buildable brick structures from 3D shapes requires more than geometric reconstruction: the output must also satisfy discrete part constraints and structural stability. Existing brick generation methods either rely on heuristic optimization, which can break down when the target 3D shape does not admit a feasible structure under predefined constraints, or generate brick sequences without explicitly modeling the underlying 3D geometry and assembly relations. In this work, we present BrickAnything, a geometry-conditioned autoregressive framework for generating buildable brick structures from diverse 3D representations. BrickAnything uses point clouds as a unified geometric interface and predicts brick sequences that reconstruct the target shape under assembly constraints. To model structural dependencies among bricks, we introduce a structure-aware tree tokenization, which represents brick structures through local attachment relations. This formulation makes sequence generation more consistent with the physical construction process, and reduces invalid intermediate states. We further introduce preference-based alignment post-training, validity-constrained decoding and adaptive rollback to improve buildability objectives such as stability and geometric fidelity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BrickAnything produces geometrically faithful and physically realizable brick structures, and that the proposed tokenization effectively reduces rollback and regeneration compared with conventional ordering strategies.

</details>


### [95] [Can LLMs Introspect? A Reality Check](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26242)
*Shashwat Singh,Tal Linzen,Shauli Ravfogel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文质疑了大语言模型（LLMs）能够检测和报告自身内部状态（即元认知监控）的现有结论。基于人类元认知研究的教训，作者指出需要区分真正的内省与基于表面线索的模式匹配。通过重新审视两个评估范式，发现模型成功源于检测异常或利用输入线索，而非真正的内省能力。引入更严格的对照实验后，模型表现接近随机水平。结论是当前证据不足以证明LLMs具备元认知监控能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究声称LLMs能够检测和报告自身内部状态，但作者认为这一结论可能过于草率。基于人类元认知研究的启示，需要区分真正的内省（genuine introspection）与基于表面线索的模式匹配（pattern matching），且单纯的行为证据不足以建立强有力的内省主张。

Method: 重新审视两个评估范式：(1) 内部状态篡改检测——对比模型区分内部状态干预与输入操作的能力；(2) 隐藏状态标签预测——比较模型基于自身隐藏状态的预测与仅基于输入的分类器性能，并引入重新标注的对照控制设置。

Result: (1) 模型无法可靠区分内部状态干预与输入操作，成功反映的是异常检测能力而非特定内省。(2) 仅访问输入的分类器能达到与模型内省预测相当的性能；在重新标注的控制设置中（模型无法依赖任务语义），模型表现接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 当前证据不足以证明LLMs具备元认知监控能力（metacognitive monitoring）。需要更严格的实验设计来区分真正的内省与模式匹配，单纯行为证据无法建立强有力的内省主张。

Abstract: Can large language models detect and report their own internal states? A number of studies have argued that the answer to this question is yes. We argue, based on lessons from human metacognition research, that this conclusion may be premature: to be convinced of this conclusion we need to distinguish genuine introspection from pattern matching based on surface-level cues. Furthermore, we argue that behavioral evidence alone is inherently insufficient to establish strong introspective claims.
  We re-examine two recently introduced evaluation paradigms in light of this consideration. In the first paradigm, models are expected to detect whether their internal states have been tampered with. We find that models cannot reliably distinguish such interventions on their internal states from manipulations of the input, suggesting that their success in the original studies reflects their ability to detect anomalies more generally, as opposed to interventions on their internal states in particular. In the second paradigm we examine, models are tasked with predicting labels derived from their own hidden states. Here, we find that classifiers that only have access to the input achieve equivalent performance to the model's own in-context predictions, indicating that the original results do not conclusively demonstrate that the model has privileged access to its internal representations. We further introduce a relabeled control setting, where models cannot rely on the semantics of the task to solve it, and instead must rely on the internal representation; models perform closer to chance on this better-controlled version of the task. Taken together, these results indicate that current evidence is insufficient to establish that LLMs display metacognitive monitoring.

</details>


### [96] [Your Agents Are Aging Too: Agent Lifespan Engineering for Deployed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26302)
*Jianing Zhu,Yeonju Ro,John Robertson,Kevin Wang,Junbo Li,Haris Vikalo,Aditya Akella,Zhangyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AgingBench基准，用于评估AI智能体在部署后的长期可靠性衰退问题，通过四种老化机制和诊断工具发现智能体性能衰退是多维度的，需要生命周期评估和针对性修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体评估仅关注部署首日的性能快照，忽略了长期运行中因交互历史压缩、记忆检索、事实修订和维护等操作导致的有效状态持续变化，缺乏对智能体全生命周期可靠性的系统评估方法。

Method: 构建AgingBench基准，定义四种老化机制（压缩老化、干扰老化、修订老化、维护老化），使用时序依赖图和配对反事实探针生成诊断画像，对记忆流水线的写入、检索和利用三个阶段进行故障定位。

Result: 在7个场景、14个模型、多种记忆策略下进行约400次运行（8-200轮次），发现：智能体老化并非单一维度；行为测试正常但事实精度可能下降；派生状态追踪可能在单个模型内急剧崩溃；相同错误答案可能因诊断画像不同而需要不同修复。

Conclusion: 可靠AI智能体部署需要生命周期评估、机制级诊断和阶段针对性修复，而非仅依赖更强的初始模型。

Abstract: Long-lived AI agents are increasingly deployed as persistent operational systems, yet they are still evaluated like freshly initialized models. Day-one benchmarks miss a basic systems question: how long does an agent remain reliable after deployment? Even when model weights are frozen, an agent's effective state keeps changing as it compresses interaction history, retrieves from a growing memory store, revises facts after updates, and undergoes routine maintenance. Reliability therefore becomes a lifespan property of the full agent harness, not only a snapshot property of the base model. We introduce AgingBench, a longitudinal reliability benchmark for agent lifespan engineering: measuring not only whether deployed agents degrade, but what form the degradation takes and where repair should target. AgingBench organizes agent aging into four mechanisms: compression aging, interference aging, revision aging, and maintenance aging. To diagnose these failures, AgingBench uses temporal dependency graphs and paired counterfactual probes that produce diagnostic profiles for the write, retrieval, and utilization stages of the memory pipeline. Across 7 scenarios, 14 models, multiple memory policies, and both runner-controlled and autonomous agents, over ~400 runs spanning 8 - 200 sessions show that agent aging is not one-dimensional: behavioral tests can remain clean while factual precision decays; derived-state tracking can collapse sharply within a single model; and the same wrong answer can require different repairs depending on what the diagnostic profile points to. These results suggest that reliable agent deployment requires lifespan evaluation, mechanism-level diagnosis, and stage-targeted repair, not only stronger day-one models.

</details>


### [97] [Experiments in Agentic AI for Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26305)
*Judy Fox,Geoffrey Fox*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了两个基于混合本地-云端架构（Google Colab）的自主AI智能体框架：DeepTS/DeepCollector用于大规模时间序列数据集的自动化整理与去重，DeepScribe用于将物理讲座自动转化为结构化科学报告。通过Cellular RAG、远程数据检查和分布式并发控制等系统工程方法，克服了当前LLM在上下文和推理方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统在处理科学工作流时存在上下文长度和推理能力的根本限制，需要开发能够自主执行复杂科学任务的智能体系统来弥补这一差距。

Method: 采用混合Local Body, Remote Brain架构，通过Python本地编排器调用LLM云端后端；实现Cellular RAG（细粒度属性提取）、远程数据检查和分布式并发控制等系统工程方法。

Result: DeepTS/DeepCollector实现了时间序列数据的大规模自动整理与去重；DeepScribe能够将视觉密集的物理讲座自动转换为结构化科学报告。

Conclusion: 所提出的智能体架构有效克服了当前LLM在科学工作流中的上下文和推理限制，并具有向深度知识图谱（DeepTS泛化）和高能物理（DeepQCD）领域推广的潜力。

Abstract: This paper details two novel frameworks for developing autonomous, agentic AI in scientific workflows. Both systems leverage a hybrid Local Body, Remote Brain architecture via Google Colab, utilizing Python-based local orchestrators to invoke large language model (LLM) cloud backends. The first agent, DeepTS/DeepCollector, automates the large-scale curation, extraction, and deduplication of time-series datasets. The second, DeepScribe, is an autonomous presentation analyzer that converts visually dense, mathematically complex physics lectures into structured scientific reports. Through practical systems engineering-such as granular attribute extraction (Cellular RAG), remote data inspection, and distributed concurrency controls-we demonstrate how agentic AI can overcome the context and reasoning limitations of current state-of-the-art systems to rigorously support scientific workflows. Finally, we outline a generalization of DeepTS to support deep knowledge graphs and discuss the application of this conceptual approach to high-energy physics (DeepQCD).

</details>


### [98] [Anchor: Mitigating Artifact Drift in Agent Benchmark Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26321)
*Maksim Ivanov,Abhijay Rana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Anchor——一种从领域专家规范自动生成业务工作流评估任务（含指令、环境、最优解和验证器）的pipeline，解决了传统方法中"artifact drift"导致的任务不一致问题。基于Anchor构建了ERP-Bench基准（300个长程ERP任务），实验表明前沿模型仅17.4%能达到完全最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体在长程企业业务流程任务上的训练与评估环境在真实性、可验证性和规模之间存在矛盾。传统任务创建方式中，指令、环境、oracle和验证器由松散耦合的流程分别生成，经常对任务要求产生分歧，导致环境不可解、可被奖励欺骗或不一致——本文称之为"artifact drift"问题。

Method: 提出Anchor任务生成pipeline，将领域专家对业务工作流的规范形式化为约束优化程序。从单一参数化规范出发，联合生成自然语言指令、环境配置、求解器证明的最优解以及基于状态的验证器。通过调整参数即可生成具有可控难度和已知最优解的新任务，产生与评估框架无关的环境，奖励仅取决于最终状态的业务正确性。

Result: 基于Anchor构建ERP-Bench基准，包含300个跨采购和制造工作流的长程任务（使用生产级ERP系统）。实验发现：生成参数可预测实际难度；前沿模型在26.1%的试验中满足显式任务约束，但仅17.4%达到完全最优解。

Conclusion: Anchor和ERP-Bench为构建可审计的、具有经济价值的智能体评估环境提供了具体方案。代码和数据集已开源在erpbench.ai。

Abstract: AI agents are beginning to complete valuable, long-horizon business operations tasks, but training and evaluation environments for enterprise work still struggle to balance realism, verifiability, and scale. Environment and task creation frequently suffers from a failure mode we call artifact drift: when instructions, environments, oracles, and verifiers are created by loosely coupled processes, they frequently disagree on what a task requires, producing environments that are unsolvable, reward-hackable, or inconsistent. We introduce Anchor, a task-generation pipeline that formalizes domain experts' specifications of business workflows into constraint optimization programs. From a single parametric specification, the pipeline jointly produces a natural-language instruction, environment configuration, solver-certified ground-truth solution, and state-based verifier. With Anchor, altering parameters yields new tasks with controlled difficulty and known optimal solutions, producing harness-agnostic environments whose rewards depend solely on end-state business correctness. We apply Anchor to produce ERP-Bench: a benchmark of 300 long-horizon tasks spanning procurement and manufacturing workflows in a production-grade ERP system. We find that generation parameters predict realized difficulty, and that frontier models satisfy explicit task constraints in 26.1% of trials but reach a fully optimal solution in only 17.4% of trials. Overall, we show that Anchor and ERP-Bench offer a concrete recipe for building auditable evaluation environments for economically valuable agent work. We release the task generator and ERP-Bench dataset at erpbench.ai

</details>


### [99] [OmniToM: Benchmarking Theory of Mind in LLMs via Explicit Belief Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26322)
*Adam Bawatneh,Sagar Sapkota,Amrit Singh Bedi,Santu Karmaker,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OmniToM是一个通过显式建模信念结构来评估大语言模型心智理论能力的新基准，包含信念提取和信念标注两阶段。基于895个故事构建了22,343个标注信念命题，揭示了当前LLM在将叙事事实转化为角色信念和共享心理状态时存在瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ToM评估仅依赖端点问答，无法判断模型是否真正构建了稳健推理所需的心智状态表征，特别是在涉及分歧、演变或错误信念的场景中。

Method: OmniToM采用两阶段评估：Stage 1信念提取——从故事中提取与社会动态相关的信念；Stage 2信念标注——为每个信念分配七维模式标签（递归阶次、真值状态、知识访问、明确性、内容类型、心理来源、上下文）。基于ToMBench的895个故事，通过人类校准的LLM辅助标注流程构建22,343个标注信念命题。

Result: 零样本评估显示，当前LLM存在角色特定的信念追踪瓶颈，特别是在将叙事事实转化为角色信念和共享心理状态所需的知识访问和表征决策方面表现不佳。

Conclusion: OmniToM揭示了现有ToM评估的局限，证明了显式信念结构建模对于评估LLM真正的心智推理能力至关重要，并指出了当前模型在信念追踪方面的具体瓶颈。

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM), the ability to infer others' knowledge, intentions, and emotions, is commonly evaluated in large language models (LLMs) using end-point question answering, where performance is judged solely by the final answer to a social reasoning query. This paradigm obscures whether the model actually constructs the underlying mental-state representations required for robust reasoning, particularly in scenarios involving divergent, evolving, or mistaken beliefs. In order to address this research gap, we introduce OmniToM, a benchmark that directly evaluates these representations by requiring explicit modeling of belief structures for all relevant actors within a narrative. These structures are composed of belief propositions: minimal statements of what an actor takes to be true about the world or another actor's mental state, allowing knowledge, intentions, emotions, and false beliefs to be analyzed in a common format. Models are evaluated in two stages: Stage 1: Belief Extraction, which extracts from the story the beliefs relevant to its social dynamics, and Stage 2: Belief Labeling, which assigns each belief a seven-dimensional schema label covering recursive order, truth status, knowledge access, explicitness, content type, mental source, and context. Built from 895 stories from the existing ToMBench story corpus and augmented with 22,343 labeled belief propositions, OmniToM uses a human-calibrated LLM-assisted annotation pipeline. Across diverse models in zero-shot evaluation, OmniToM reveals an actor-specific belief-tracking bottleneck: current LLMs struggle with the knowledge-access and representational decisions required to transform narrative facts into actors' beliefs and shared mental states.

</details>


### [100] [Managing Uncertainty in LLM-Generated Procedural Knowledge for Virtual Laboratory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26333)
*Polychronis Karpodinis,Dimitris Kalles*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个原型框架，用于管理大语言模型生成虚拟实验室流程时产生的程序性不确定性。该框架通过结构化领域表示和LLM生成的状态转换样本，提取候选程序规则，将其转化为可检查的约束，并修复不确定的程序步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟实验室中，教育工作者手动编写实验流程成本高昂，LLM可辅助生成但输出不可靠（遗漏步骤、顺序错误、逻辑不正确），因此需要管理LLM生成程序知识中的不确定性。

Method: 使用结构化领域表示和LLM生成的不确定状态转换样本，提取候选程序规则，将其转化为显式可检查的约束，并利用这些约束修复不确定的程序步骤。

Result: 在涉及实验仪器、容器、工具和物质转移操作的虚拟实验室领域中展示了该方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅适用于教育虚拟实验室，更可推广到结构化交互环境中管理不确定程序知识以进行行动规划的通用问题。

Abstract: Educational virtual laboratories can make experimental training more scala-ble, adaptive, and accessible, especially when students have limited access to physical laboratory facilities. However, authoring new simulated laboratory procedures remains costly: educators must describe new equipment, define how instruments and materials interact, and specify valid procedural flows that can be executed or assessed inside the virtual environment. Large lan-guage models can assist in this authoring process by generating detailed ex-perimental procedures, but their output should not be treated as directly exe-cutable plans. They may omit necessary actions, arrange steps in the wrong order, or produce instructions that are logically incorrect or incompatible with the laboratory equipment. This paper presents a prototype framework for managing uncertainty in LLM-generated procedural knowledge for virtu-al laboratory planning. The framework aims to reduce procedural uncertainty by using structured domain representations and uncertain LLM-generated state-transition samples to extract candidate procedural rules, transform them into explicit and inspectable constraints, and use them to repair uncertain procedural steps. Although the motivating domain refers to educational vir-tual laboratories, the underlying problem is more general: managing uncer-tain procedural knowledge for action planning in structured interactive envi-ronments. We illustrate the approach in a virtual laboratory domain involving laboratory instruments, containers, tools, and material-transfer actions.

</details>


### [101] [Automatic Layer Selection for Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26366)
*Xinpeng Wang,William Cao,Andrew Gordon Wilson,Zhe Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型幻觉检测中的中间层选择问题，提出了一种无需训练的自动层选择准则FEPoID（First Effective Peak of Intrinsic Dimension），该准则通过识别内在维度的第一个有效峰值来选择最优或近优层，并引入了简单的截断策略进一步增强幻觉相关信号，在多项基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明幻觉相关信号在LLM中间层比最终层更强烈，但如何自动选择高性能层的问题尚未被充分探索，缺乏原则性的方法。

Method: 首先提出多种关于中间层幻觉信号涌现的假设并评估对应准则，发现均无法一致取得满意效果；随后提出FEPoID准则，基于内在维度（Intrinsic Dimension）的第一个有效峰值选择最优层；同时引入一种简单的生成截断策略来增强幻觉信号。

Result: FEPoID能够一致地识别最优或近优层，在问答和摘要两类幻觉检测基准上，性能优于所提出的其他准则以及现有幻觉检测基线方法，且计算开销可忽略不计。所提出的截断策略进一步显著提升了整体检测性能。

Conclusion: FEPoID是一种高效、无需训练且普适性强的自动层选择方法，结合截断策略可有效提升LLM幻觉检测性能。

Abstract: Recent studies on hallucination detection have shown that hallucination-related signals are more strongly encoded in intermediate layers than in the final layer of large language models (LLMs). Although a growing body of work has sought to exploit this property for hallucination detection, how to automate the selection of high-performing layers remains underexplored, and principled methods for this purpose are still lacking. To address this gap, we first propose several hypotheses for why such signals emerge in intermediate layers and evaluate corresponding criteria for automatic layer selection across diverse LLM architectures, scales, and tasks, covering both question answering and summarization hallucination detection benchmarks. However, we find that none of these criteria consistently delivers satisfactory performance. We therefore propose a new selection criterion, First Effective Peak of Intrinsic Dimension (FEPoID), which consistently identify optimal or near-optimal layers and outperforms both the aforementioned criteria and existing hallucination detection baselines. FEPoID is training-free and incurs negligible computational overhead. In addition, we study the generation behaviors of LLMs and introduce a simple yet effective truncation strategy, which further amplifies hallucination-related signals and substantially improves overall detection performance. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/DesoloYw/Automatic-Layer-Selection-for-Hallucination-Detection.git

</details>


### [102] [Exploiting Local Dynamics Regularity for Reusable Skills in Offline Hierarchical RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26371)
*Sarthak Dayal,Abhinav Peri,Carl Qi,Claas Voelcker,Alexander Levine,Caleb Chuck,Amy Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CARL算法，通过对比学习对齐局部上下文与所需动作序列，实现可复用技能发现，在复杂人形环境和OGBench基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层强化学习（HRL）虽承诺通过发现和复用时间扩展技能来解决长视界任务，但获取真正可复用的技能仍是一个开放挑战。

Method: 利用局部动态直觉——不同全局上下文中的局部转换需要相似的动作序列——通过对齐上下文与所需动作序列，学习哪些技能可复用及在何处复用。具体实现为对比式基于动作的表征学习（CARL）。

Result: 在复杂人形环境中实现了有意义技能的质量聚类，与HIQL集成后在OGBench基准上提升了下游任务性能。

Conclusion: 通过对齐局部上下文与动作序列来学习可复用技能，能有效提升HRL算法的性能，且该方法具有通用性，可集成到现有HRL算法中。

Abstract: Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (HRL) promises to solve long-horizon Reinforcement Learning (RL) tasks more efficiently than non-hierarchical counterparts by discovering and reusing temporally-extended skills. However, obtaining skills that are actually reusable remains an open challenge. Towards this end, we focus on abstractions that exploit the intuition of local dynamics: local transitions in different global contexts require similar kinds of action sequences. By aligning these contexts with the action sequences they require, we are able to learn which skills to reuse and where to reuse them. In principle, this information should benefit many HRL algorithms, where high-level policies have to reason about the low-level skills they use. The resulting algorithm CARL (Contrastive Action-based Representations for Reusable Local Control) shows both qualitative clustering of meaningful skills in complex humanoid environments and improved downstream performance on the OGBench benchmark when integrated with HIQL.

</details>


### [103] [Advancing Creative Physical Intelligence in Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26396)
*Cheng Qian,Hyeonjeong Ha,Jiayu Liu,Jeonghwan Kim,Emre Can Acikgoz,Bingxuan Li,Kunlun Zhu,Jiateng Liu,Aditi Tiwari,Zhenhailong Wang,Xiusi Chen,Mahdi Namazifar,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MM-CreativityBench基准，用于评估多模态大模型在视觉丰富的物理约束环境中进行具身创造性工具使用的能力，并针对模型缺乏持续接地探索的问题，提出了基于偏好的具身接地对齐方法，通过直接偏好优化和知识库监督显著降低了幻觉和接地错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LMMs在感知和推理方面取得进展，但尚不清楚这些能力是否能泛化到开放环境中发现视觉接地解决方案的创造性问题解决任务中。当前基准测试主要关注回答定义明确的问题，缺乏对创造性工具使用这一人类智能核心能力的评估。

Method: 1) 构建MM-CreativityBench基准，包含场景图像和候选实体结构化视图，支持细粒度交互式评估；2) 提出具身接地对齐方法，将创造性工具使用建模为偏好学习问题，使用DPO鼓励模型偏好基于视觉证据的属性-具身推理；3) 引入从具身知识库中推导的监督信号，引导实体探索和多轮规划。

Result: 当前LMMs在创造性工具使用任务中表现不佳，主要原因不是生成能力不足，而是缺乏持续的接地探索——模型常忽略相关实体、对关键部分检查不足，或产生图像中不存在的幻觉属性。所提出的方法在选择正确实体和部件方面取得一致提升，同时大幅减少了幻觉和接地相关错误。

Conclusion: 创造性问题解决（即识别场景元素如何被以非显而易见但物理可行方式重新利用）是当前多模态大模型面临的关键挑战，通过将接地对齐作为偏好学习问题处理，可以有效提升模型在视觉接地创造性推理中的表现。

Abstract: Large multimodal models (LMMs) have rapidly advanced in perception and reasoning; however, it remains unclear whether these capabilities generalize to discovering visually grounded solutions in open-ended environments, beyond pattern recognition. In such settings, intelligence requires more than answering well-posed questions: it involves identifying how elements in a scene can be repurposed in non-obvious yet physically feasible ways. This form of creative problem-solving is central to human intelligence, but remains largely untested in current benchmarks. To evaluate this ability, we introduce MM-CreativityBench, a benchmark for affordance-grounded creative tool use in visually rich, physically constrained environments. Each instance presents a scenario image with structured views of candidate entities and their parts, enabling fine-grained, interactive evaluation of how models iteratively inspect the scene, identify relevant affordances, and compose visually and physically grounded solutions. Our experiments show that current LMMs often fall short, not due to lack of generative capability, but because they do not sustain grounded exploration. Models often overlook relevant entities, under-examine critical parts, or hallucinate attributes not grounded in the image. Motivated by this failure mode, we propose affordance-grounded alignment, which casts creative tool use as a preference learning problem. Using Direct Preference Optimization, we encourage models to prefer attribute-affordance reasoning grounded in visual evidence over hallucinated alternatives. In addition, we incorporate supervision derived from an affordance knowledge base to guide broader entity exploration and multi-turn planning. Our results show consistent gains in selecting the correct entities and parts, while substantially reducing hallucination and grounding-related errors.

</details>


### [104] [The MiniMax-M2 Series: Mini Activations Unleashing Max Real-World Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26494)
*MiniMax,:,Aili Chen,Aonian Li,Baichuan Zhou,Bangwei Gong,Binyang Jiang,Boji Dan,Changqing Yu,Chao Wang,Cheng Ma,Cheng Zhong,Cheng Zhu,Chengjun Xiao,Chengyi Yang,Chengyu Du,Chenyang Zhang,Chi Zhang,Chuangyi Huang,Chunhao Zhang,Chunhui Du,Chunyu Zhao,Congchao Guo,Da Chen,Deming Ding,Dianjun Sun,Dongyu Zhang,Enhui Yang,Fei Yu,Guang Zheng,Guodong Zheng,Guohong Li,Haichao Zhu,Haigang Zhou,Haimo Zhang,Han Ding,Hao Zhang,Haohai Sun,Haolin Lyu,Haonan Lu,Haoyu Wang,Huajie Shi,Huiyang Li,Jiacheng Chen,Jian Zhang,Jiaqi Zhuang,Jiaren Cai,Jiaxin Pan,Jiayao Li,Jiayuan Song,Jichuan Zhang,Jie Wang,Jihao Gu,Jin Zhu,Jingwei Dong,Jingyang Li,Jingyu Zhang,Jingze Zhuang,Jinhao Tian,Jinli Liu,Jinyi Hu,Jun Tao,Jun Zhang,Junbin Ruan,Junhao Xu,Junjie Yan,Junteng Liu,Junxian He,Kang Xu,Ke Ji,Ke Yang,Kecheng Xiao,Keyu Duan,Keyu Li,Le Han,Letian Ruan,Li Yuan,Lianfei Yu,Liheng Feng,Lijie Mo,Lin Li,Lingye Bao,Lingyu Yang,Lingyuan Zhou,Loki,Lu Chen,Lunbin Ceng,Ming Li,Ming Zhong,Mingliang Tao,Mingyuan Chi,Mujie Lin,Nan Hu,Ningxin Chen,Peiyin Zhu,Peng Gao,Pengcheng Gao,Pengfei Li,Penglin Li,Pengyu Zhao,Qibin Ren,Qidi Xu,Qihan Ren,Qile Li,Qin Wang,Quanliang Chen,Qunhong Ceng,Rong Tian,Rui Dong,Ruitao Leng,Ruize Zhang,Shanqi Liu,Shaoyu Chen,Sheng Jia,Shun Yao,Shuoran Zhao,Shuqi Yu,Sichen Li,Sicheng Pan,Songquan Zhu,Tengfei Li,Tian Xie,Tiancheng Qin,Tianrun Liang,Wei Liu,Weiqi Xu,Weitao Li,Weixiang Chen,Weiyu Cheng,Weiyu Zhang,Wenhu Chen,Wenqian Zhao,Xiancai Chen,Xiangjun Song,Xiangyuan Wang,Xiao Luo,Xiao Su,Xiaobo Li,Xiaodong Han,Xiaojie Wu,Xihao Song,Xingyi Han,Xinyu Guan,Xuan Lu,Xun Zou,Xunhao Lai,Xutong Li,Yan Gong,Yang Wang,Yang Xu,Yangsen Wang,Ye Tang,Yicheng Chen,Yinran Qiu,Yiqi Shi,Yiting Guo,Yiwen Huang,Yixuan Wang,Yongyi Hu,Yu Gao,Yu Zhang,Yuanxiang Ying,Yuanzhen Zhang,Yubo Wang,Yuchen Song,Yufeng Yang,Yuhang Meng,Yuhang Miao,Yuhao Li,Yujie Liu,Yulin Hu,Yunan Huang,Yunji Li,Yunyi Huang,Yusen Zhang,Yusu Hong,Yutao Xie,Yutong Zhang,Yuwen Liao,Yuxuan Shi,Yuze Wenren,Zebin Li,Zehan Li,Zejian Luo,Zeyu Jin,Zeyuan Sun,Zhanpeng Zhou,Zhaochen Su,Zhendong Li,Zhengmao Zhu,Zhengyuan Peng,Zhenhua Fan,Zhi Zhang,Zhichao Xu,Zhiheng Lv,Zhikang Xu,Zhitao He,Zhiwei He,Zhongyuan Li,Zibo Gao,Zijia Wu,Zijian Song,Zijian Zhou,Zijun Sun,Zishan Huang,Ziying Chen,Ziyue Ge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MiniMax-M2系列是基于混合专家架构的语言模型，以229.9B总参数量、仅9.8B激活参数实现前沿性能，核心依赖智能体驱动数据管线、Forge强化学习系统和自我进化机制三大组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在极小激活参数条件下实现最大实际智能的可能性，为智能体部署场景设计高效的端到端解决方案。

Method: (i) 智能体驱动数据管线：在大规模可验证轨迹上训练，涵盖智能体编程和协同工作，基于可执行工作空间和artifact对齐奖励；(ii) Forge系统：可扩展的智能体原生RL系统，支持长程轨迹，采用窗口FIFO调度、前缀树合并、推理优化及训练-推理-智能体解耦；(iii) M2.7检查点：实现早期自我进化，自动调试训练运行并修改自身scaffold。

Result: 在智能体编程、深度搜索、办公任务和推理基准上达到前沿性能，以极小激活参数实现与更大模型相当的表现。

Conclusion: Mini激活（Mini activations）可以释放最大实际智能（Maximum real-world intelligence），为高效智能体模型设计提供了可扩展的范式。

Abstract: We introduce the MiniMax-M2 series, a family of Mixture-of-Experts language models built around the principle that mini activations can unleash maximum real-world intelligence. The flagship M2 contains 229.9B total parameters with only 9.8B activated per token. Designed end-to-end for agentic deployment, the M2 series rests on three components: (i) agent-driven data pipelines producing large-scale, verifiable trajectories across agentic coding and agentic cowork, each grounded in an executable workspace and an artifact-aligned reward; (ii) Forge, a scalable agent-native RL system that adapts to long-horizon agent trajectories, paired with windowed-FIFO scheduling, prefix-tree merging, inference optimization, and a clean training-inference-agent decoupling that supports both white-box and black-box agents; (iii) the latest M2.7 checkpoint takes an early step toward self-evolution -- autonomously debugging training runs and modifying its own scaffold. Across M2 through M2.7, this combination translates a mini-activation footprint into frontier-tier performance on agentic coding, deep search, office-task, and reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [105] [PolyFusionAgent: A Multimodal Foundation Model and Autonomous AI Assistant for Polymer Property Prediction and Inverse Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26543)
*Manpreet Kaur,Xingying Zhang,Qian Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PolyFusionAgent是一个交互式聚合物发现框架，结合了多模态聚合物基础模型PolyFusion和文献驱动的设计智能体PolyAgent，通过统一序列、拓扑、3D几何和指纹等多种视图，实现聚合物性质预测、条件生成和基于文献证据的闭环设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合物设计受困于巨大的化学设计空间以及结构、性质、先验知识的碎片化表示，导致许多AI模型与物理和实验现实脱节，无法支持可直接操作的设计决策。

Method: 提出PolyFusionAgent框架，包含两个核心组件：1) PolyFusion——多模态聚合物基础模型，对齐序列、拓扑、3D几何和指纹等多种视图，学习跨化学空间和数据 regimes 的共享潜在空间；2) PolyAgent——工具增强的文献基础设计智能体，通过证据检索从聚合物文献中获取支持，实现假设提出、评估和上下文化。

Result: PolyFusion改进了热物理性质预测，实现了超出参考设计空间的化学有效且结构新颖的聚合物的性质条件生成。PolyAgent通过文献证据检索将预测和逆设计与可验证的科学推理连接起来。

Conclusion: PolyFusionAgent实现了交互式、证据链接的聚合物发现，结合了大规模表征学习、多模态化学知识和可验证的科学推理，构建了完整的设计闭环。

Abstract: Polymer discovery is central to fields ranging from energy storage to biomedicine, but it is hindered by an astronomically large chemical design space and fragmented representations of structure, properties, and prior knowledge. This fragmentation leaves many AI models disconnected from physical and experimental reality, restricting their ability to support directly actionable design decisions. Here we introduce PolyFusionAgent, an interactive framework coupling a multimodal polymer foundation model (PolyFusion) with a tool-augmented, literature-grounded design agent (PolyAgent). PolyFusion aligns complementary polymer views including sequence, topology, 3D geometry, and fingerprints across millions of polymers to learn a shared latent space transferable across chemistries and data regimes, improving thermophysical property prediction and enabling property-conditioned generation of chemically valid, structurally novel polymers beyond the reference design space. PolyAgent closes the design loop by linking prediction and inverse design with evidence retrieval from the polymer literature, proposing, evaluating, and contextualizing hypotheses with explicit precedent in one workflow. Together, PolyFusionAgent enables interactive, evidence-linked polymer discovery combining large-scale representation learning, multimodal chemical knowledge, and verifiable scientific reasoning.

</details>


### [106] [MedGuideX: Internalizing Decision Logic from Executable Guidelines into Large Language Models for Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26567)
*Yuhao Shen,Lang Cao,Simo Du,Yuqing Wang,Juexiao Zhou,Hao Peng,Yue Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从临床实践指南（CPG）中提取可执行决策逻辑，生成事实性与反事实性问答数据来微调医学大语言模型的方法，得到的MedGuideX在四个临床推理基准上平均准确率提升10.28%，并在医生评估中在忠实性、有效性、完整性和清晰度方面优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅将CPG作为自由文本训练数据或检索来源，未充分利用其程序化决策结构，导致模型不能深入理解指南支持的决策逻辑及不同患者条件下的决策变化。

Method: 提出一种指南派生训练管道：将CPG推荐转化为可执行临床决策逻辑，据此生成事实性和反事实性问答数据，再用这些数据对医学大语言模型进行后训练，得到MedGuideX。

Result: 在四个临床推理基准上，MedGuideX平均准确率相对提升10.28%；医生评估显示其在推理步骤还原以及忠实性、有效性、完整性和清晰度方面均优于基线。

Conclusion: CPG中的可执行决策逻辑可转化为可扩展的监督信号，用于构建更可靠的医学大语言模型。

Abstract: Clinical practice guidelines (CPGs) encode evidence-based decision logic that clinicians apply by evaluating patient variables, conditional criteria, and recommendation rules. However, existing methods often use CPGs as free-text training data or retrieval sources, underutilizing their procedural decision structure. To better exploit this structure, we introduce a guideline-derived training pipeline that transforms CPG recommendations into executable clinical decision logic and uses it to generate factual and counterfactual question-answering data. Theses data teach models both guideline-supported decisions and how decisions change under different patient conditions. Post-training a medical LLM on the generated data yields MedGuideX. Across four clinical reasoning benchmarks, MedGuideX achieves a 10.28% relative improvement in average accuracy. Physician evaluation further shows that MedGuideX better recovers clinician authored reasoning steps and produces physician-preferred rationales in faithfulness, validity, completeness, and clarity. Overall, our results show that executable decision logic from CPGs can be transformed into scalable supervision for building reliable medical LLMs.

</details>


### [107] [AGORA: Adapter-Grounded Observation-Action Retention for Inference-Free Prompt Compression in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26596)
*Haoran Zhang,Zhaohua Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 面向LLM Agent的token级抽取式压缩器存在结构性问题——会破坏动作语法（删除携带动作语义的低自信息量token），导致收益崩溃。本文提出AGORA，一种推理无关的步骤级压缩器，结合结构提示解析器、格式/时序关键内容保留层和基于反事实动作标签训练的125M相关度评分器，在9个测试单元中8个保持≥75%的无压缩性能，且实现1.0-11.5x自适应端到端压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的token级抽取式压缩器虽然能实现1.3-13.3x的压缩率，但在17个(环境,主干,方法)组合中，每个组合的平均奖励均不超过0.05，说明这些通用压缩器在结构上不适合LLM Agent场景。需要分析这一失败模式的根本原因并设计专门针对Agent的压缩方法。

Method: 提出AGORA，包含三个组件：1）结构提示解析器，识别动作语法结构；2）始终保留层（always-keep floor），保护格式关键内容和时序关键内容；3）125M参数的相关度评分器，基于反事实动作变更标签（counterfactual next-action-change labels）训练，每步仅需~2ms，无需LLM调用。压缩粒度从token级提升到step级。

Result: 在9个测试单元中（环境与方法和模型组合），AGORA是唯一在8个单元中保持≥75%无压缩性能的方法，唯一例外为73%。相比其他推理无关方法和基于LLM的方法，AGORA表现最佳。四路组件消融实验表明，结构保留层是质量的关键杠杆，学习型评分器在单一固定保持率下实现了1.0-11.5x的自适应端到端压缩。

Conclusion: token级通用压缩器不适合Agent场景，根本原因在于动作语法破坏（action-grammar destruction）：具有动作语义的token（标识符、括号、动作动词）恰好是自信息量最低的，通用压缩器会优先删除它们，导致环境拒绝执行。step级压缩是解决方案，AGORA通过结构感知设计有效解决了这一问题。

Abstract: The token-level extractive compressors widely used for general LM context are structurally inappropriate for LLM agents: across 17 (env, backbone, method) cells spanning two independent token-level method families, every cell collapses to mean reward <= 0.05 despite 1.3-13.3x realized compression. We name and characterize this failure mode as action-grammar destruction -- the tokens carrying action semantics (identifiers, brackets, action verbs) are exactly those self-information ranks lowest, so a general-purpose compressor reliably removes them and the environment rejects the residual. The diagnosis points to step-granularity compression. We introduce AGORA, an inference-free step-level compressor combining a structural prompt parser, an always-keep floor for format- and recency-critical content, and a 125M-parameter relevance scorer trained on counterfactual next-action-change labels (~2ms/step, zero per-step LLM toll). Across the compared inference-free and LLM-based methods, AGORA is the only one retaining >= 75% uncompressed performance in 8 of 9 cells (with the lone exception at 73%); a four-way component ablation isolates the structural floor as the dominant quality lever and the learned scorer as the source of 1.0-11.5x adaptive end-to-end compression from a single fixed keep ratio.

</details>


### [108] [Tail-Aware HiFloat4: W4A4 Post-Training Quantization for Wan2.2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26628)
*Zhanfeng Feng,Shuai Guo,Xin Di,Long Peng,Yang Cao,Zhengjun Zha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Tail-Aware HiFloat4方法，针对Wan2.2模型在低比特文本到视频生成量化挑战中，采用HiFloat4数值格式对主线性层进行W4A4伪量化，保留边界模块高精度，并引入激活尾感知的分位数校准模块构建通道掩码，结合紧凑的PTQ状态恢复，减少罕见校准异常值的影响，同时保持运行时HiFloat4算术和采样流程不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低比特文本到视频生成量化挑战中，需要对Wan2.2模型进行高效量化，同时减少罕见校准异常值对量化精度的影响，保持运行时HiFloat4算术和采样流程不变。

Method: 1. 基于ViDiT-Q后训练量化流程，适配Wan2.2模型至HiFloat4数值格式；2. 对Wan2.2两个Transformer模块中的主线性层进行W4A4 HiFloat4伪量化；3. 保持数值敏感的边界模块高精度；4. 引入激活尾感知的分位数校准模块（activation-tail-aware percentile calibration）用于通道掩码构建；5. 结合紧凑的PTQ状态恢复。

Result: 成功减少罕见校准异常值的影响，同时保持运行时HiFloat4算术和采样流程不变，应用于低比特文本到视频生成量化挑战。

Conclusion: Tail-Aware HiFloat4方法通过激活尾感知的校准和紧凑的PTQ状态恢复，有效提升了低比特文本到视频生成模型的量化性能。

Abstract: This report describes Tail-Aware HiFloat4, our submission to the low-bit text-to-video generation quantization challenge. Our method adapts the public ViDiT-Q post-training quantization pipeline to Wan2.2 under the HiFloat4 numerical format. We quantize the main linear layers in both Wan2.2 transformer modules with W4A4 HiFloat4 fake quantization, keep numerically sensitive boundary modules in high precision, and introduce an activation-tail-aware percentile calibration module for channel-mask construction. Together with compact PTQ-state restoration, this design reduces the influence of rare calibration outliers while keeping the runtime HiFloat4 arithmetic and sampling pipeline unchanged.

</details>


### [109] [UnityMAS-O: A General RL Optimization Framework for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26646)
*Yiqun Chen,Wei Yang,Erhan Zhang,Shijie Wang,Qi Liu,Zechun Niu,Bin Zhang,Haitao Li,Rui Li,Lingyong Yan,Jinyuan Feng,Biqing Qi,Xiaochi Wei,Yan Gao,Yi Wu,Yao Hu,Jiaxin Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UnityMAS-O是一个通用的强化学习优化框架，将整个LLM多智能体工作流作为优化单元，支持逻辑角色与物理模型解耦、多层级奖励分配，并通过Ray-based星型拓扑运行时实现分布式PPO更新，在检索增强QA、迭代搜索和代码生成等任务上显著提升了多智能体系统的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体系统主要依赖手工编排（提示词、工具、控制规则），缺乏统一的强化学习优化接口；现有RL后训练框架仅针对单策略优化，缺少对用户定义的多智能体工作流、结构化交互、角色级信用分配和可配置参数共享的支持。

Method: 将完整工作流作为优化单元；通过四个一级对象（逻辑智能体角色、图轨迹、用户定义奖励、智能体-模型映射）解耦逻辑智能体与物理模型参数；支持全共享、全分离、部分共享三种参数共享模式；角色/轮次/轨迹三个层级分配奖励；基于Ray的星型拓扑运行时（中央控制器+模型本地Worker组）实现分布式PPO更新。

Result: 在Natural Questions、HotpotQA和代码生成任务上，多智能体RL优化后手工指定的工作流性能提升，小模型和严格代码全通过指标上增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: UnityMAS-O可作为可复用的基础框架，将多样化的LLM多智能体工作流转化为可训练的多智能体RL系统。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems decompose complex tasks into interacting roles, but most remain manually orchestrated by prompts, tools, and control rules, while agents are rarely optimized through a unified reinforcement learning interface. Existing RL post-training frameworks mainly target single-policy optimization and lack abstractions for user-defined multi-agent workflows, structured interaction, role-specific credit assignment, and configurable parameter sharing.
  We present UnityMAS-O, a general RL optimization framework for LLM-based multi-agent systems. UnityMAS-O treats the complete workflow as the optimization unit, rather than a single response or policy trajectory. It represents workflows through four first-class objects: logical agent roles, graph trajectories, user-defined rewards, and agent--model mappings. This decouples logical agents from physical model parameters, supporting full sharing, full separation, and partial sharing, with rewards assigned at role, turn, and trajectory levels.
  UnityMAS-O extends verl with a Ray-based star-topology runtime. A central controller executes workflows, invokes tools, records structured trajectories, and assembles rewards; model-local worker groups handle rollout, buffering, advantage computation, and distributed PPO-style updates. Users can define agents, workflows, model mappings, and rewards without rewriting the optimization infrastructure.
  We instantiate UnityMAS-O on retrieval-augmented QA, iterative agentic search, and reflective code generation. Across Natural Questions, HotpotQA, and held-out code tasks, multi-agent RL improves manually specified workflows after optimization, with especially large gains for smaller models and strict code all-passed metrics. These results show that UnityMAS-O can serve as a reusable substrate for converting diverse LLM-based multi-agent workflows into trainable multi-agent RL systems.

</details>


### [110] [Completion vs Optimality: Policy Gradient in Long-Horizon Cumulative-Damage Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26657)
*Wolfgang Maass,Sabine Janzen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有累积损伤的长时域决策问题，发现策略梯度方法在该类问题上存在两种正交的失败模式：完成（到达终期而非提前退出）和最优性（匹配动态规划基准）。通过PPO算法分析，提出四种可验证预测，并在砖瓦工职业和NBA大前锋生涯两个独立校准环境中进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域决策问题中，局部吸引的动作会累积导致全局不利后果，策略梯度方法在此类问题上存在未被充分分析的失败模式。

Method: 将策略梯度方法的失败模式分解为完成和最优性两个正交维度；使用PPO算法结合线性软惩罚进行分析；推导四种可检验预测；在两个独立校准环境中进行验证。

Result: 单独开放时域访问会降低完成率；结合动作空间限制可实现完成但存在最优性差距（ΔM_final = 0.271）；所有四种预测均定性复现；时域不变性预测在四个测试时域中的三个成立。

Conclusion: 策略梯度方法在累积损伤决策问题中的两种失败模式可被分解和独立分析，通过动作空间限制和时域访问的结合可以部分解决完成问题，但最优性差距仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Long-horizon decision problems with cumulative damage couple locally attractive actions to globally adverse outcomes. We identify two orthogonal failure modes for policy-gradient methods on this class and propose a decomposition that separates them: \emph{completion} (reaching the terminal horizon rather than exiting via an implicit terminal constraint) and \emph{optimality} (matching the dynamic-programming reference given completion). Under PPO with a linear soft penalty, granting horizon access alone reduces the completion rate: the penalty's equilibrium drives the dominant-activity share to zero, while action-space restriction combined with horizon access achieves completion but leaves an optimality gap ($ΔM_{\text{final}} = 0.271$) that we trace to first-phase greedy commitment at the damage origin. We derive four testable predictions and evaluate them in two separately calibrated environments that share the same abstract structure but differ in domain, horizon, activity set, and calibration data: a 49-step bricklayer career and a 20-season NBA power-forward career. All four predictions replicate qualitatively. The horizon-invariance prediction is met at three of four tested horizons, with the exception at $H = 15$ consistent with the $H^*$ boundary ($H^* \in [6, 14]$ under the NBA parameters).

</details>


### [111] [MemFail: Stress-Testing Failure Modes of LLM Memory Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26667)
*Ishir Garg,Neel Kolhe,Dawn Song,Xuandong Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemFail是一个诊断性基准测试，通过形式化记忆系统的三个核心操作（摘要、存储、检索）并针对每个操作设计对抗性数据集，系统性地识别和隔离LLM记忆系统的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试仅报告聚合的问答准确率，将记忆系统视为黑箱，无法将错误答案归因于特定的系统失败模式。缺乏对LLM记忆系统具体故障模式和设计选择的实证理解。

Method: 形式化记忆系统为摘要、存储、检索三个规范操作的组合，识别每个操作可能导致的失败模式；基于假设的失败模式构建覆盖四个任务的五个对抗性数据集；在MemFail上评估四种最先进的记忆系统。

Result: 通过MemFail评估了四种最先进的记忆系统，展示了该基准可用于实证理解不同记忆系统架构之间的权衡。
